Command-invoked. ([RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering](https://github.com/RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-graph) · ★10 · 3 skills, 1 agent, 1 hook file)
